# MS.990 Parallel Edition: Canvases 575–604

## Page 630 — IIIF Canvas #575

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0575.JP2` (1801 × 2204 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0575.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0575.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Continuation of remedies for cradle cap / milk-crust using absorbent powders (hartshorn, crabs' eyes, coral tincture), lead liniments, tutty, and crocus martis; followed by a section on diseases children inherit from their mothers or wet-nurses, detailing the qualities of healthy versus unhealthy breast milk.

## Page 631 — IIIF Canvas #576

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0576.JP2` (1801 × 2017 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0576.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0576.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Conclusion of advice on wet-nurses' health and avoiding overfeeding, followed by a section on restless sleep and fright in children ('Von Unruehigen Schlaff. und Schröcken der Kinder') with herbal washes and plasters, and a section on children crying out in their sleep ('Wann die Kinder in schlaff auff schreyen und Erschröcken') with honey, licorice, theriac, peony seeds, and cinnabar remedies.

## Page 632 — IIIF Canvas #577

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0577.JP2` (1800 × 2199 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0577.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0577.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Early New High German pediatric medical recipes continuing remedies for colic/pain from spoiled milk, sleeplessness in children, vomiting of children (Von Erbröchen der Khinder), and children's cough (Huesten der Khinder), with rubricated headings in red ink, olive-green horizontal section dividers, and pencil foliation '286'.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | darin gebaden. hernach weissen Senf sam gestossen | bathed therein. Afterwards white mustard together with pounded |
| 2 | küm darin geweicht. über den ben woll erwarmt. | caraway soaked therein, well warmed over the limb/body. |
| 3 | ettlichen khindern wen der schmerz | To some children, when the pain |
| 4 | von verderbter milch auß dem magen khombt, ist | comes from spoiled milk out of the stomach, it is |
| 5 | sehr gutt und Notwendig das man den khind | very good and necessary that one gives the child |
| 6 | zum bröchen ein gebe. oder mit ein föderle den | something to vomit, or touches the throat with a little feather. |
| 7 | schlund berührt. ettliche nemen ein mandl öll | Some take an almond oil |
| 8 | und milch. und das föderle in selbs salbt. | and milk, and anoint the little feather in the same. |
| 9 | Oder brauch Volgente mittel | Or use the following remedies *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |
| 10 | gib den Syrupo Emeticum 15. 20. biß 50 | Give the Syrupus Emeticus, 15, 20, up to 50 |
| 11 | oder 60 dropfen. nach dem das khind stärkhe | or 60 drops, according to how much strength the child |
| 12 | hat. in Cimet ▽ geben. oder hechel aug. | has, given in cinnamon water, or restharrow/eye-bright. *— Note: Contains symbol ▽ (water)* |

| 15 | hernach khan man alß das Mägl schmieren. mit Mastix | Afterwards one can then anoint the little stomach with mastic |

## Page 633 — IIIF Canvas #578

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0578.JP2` (1801 × 2017 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0578.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0578.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

## Page 635 — IIIF Canvas #580

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0580.JP2` (1801 × 2017 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0580.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0580.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Pediatric remedies for teething pain and promoting tooth eruption, followed by cosmetic recipes for a fair, smooth, and unblemished face ('Schönes Raines angesicht', 'Jungkhfrau Milch'), and treatments for discharging and foul-smelling ears in children.

## Page 636 — IIIF Canvas #581

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0581.JP2` (1800 × 2210 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0581.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0581.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Remedies for birth-strain/hip dislocation, lameness in children, umbilical and wind-ruptures ('Nabel Bruch der Kinder', 'für die Windt brüch der Kinder'), internal and external hernia treatments, and an introductory note on belly worms in children.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Gold/ochre rectangular patch (top left)** (`#xywh=400,400,200,1000`): A horizontal gold/ochre painted or pasted rectangle in the upper left margin of the left column, covering an earlier heading or mark, adjacent to 'Leminor'.

**Reddish-brown oval medallion (top right column)** (`#xywh=400,400,200,1000`): A small reddish-brown watercolor oval in the upper left of the right column next to the rubricated heading 'Andere khöstliche mitl für die Bruch'.

## Page 640 — IIIF Canvas #585

Page 640 (fol. 290r) continues remedies for constipation in children, then covers urinary incontinence (Wan die khinder den Urin nit halten khünen), urinary retention (Wan die khinder nit harnen khünen), and emaciation/wasting in children (Mager Und Abnemung des khindes).

## Page 641 — IIIF Canvas #586

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0586.JP2` (1801 × 2017 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0586.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0586.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Pediatric remedies in Early New High German, including ointments for pustules/swellings, a strengthening herbal and milk bath for children unable to stand, treatment for 'Mitesser' (comedones/wasting worms) by lye-bathing and shaving, and dietary/cooling water regimens for fevers in infants and wet-nurses.

## Page 642 — IIIF Canvas #587

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0587.JP2` (1800 × 2210 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0587.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0587.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Continuation of pediatric fever remedies (burnt ivory decoction, cooling melon-seed drink, plantain plaster for the epigastrium, electuaries for great thirst), followed by a section on pediatric hiccups/sobbing ('schluzen') and its treatments, and the opening of a section on eye ailments in small children accompanied by an anatomical eye illustration.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Anatomical eye diagram** (`#xywh=400,400,200,1000`): Ink and grey-wash illustration at the bottom of the right column depicting a human eye in frontal view with eyebrow and nose bridge, accompanying the section 'Von den Zuefällen der Augen der kleinen Kindern'.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |

## Page 643 — IIIF Canvas #588

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0588.JP2` (1801 × 2017 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0588.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0588.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Remedies for pediatric eye pain, redness, bloodshot eyes, and discharge, followed by a major section on epilepsy and convulsions ('hinfallent und fraiß') in small children, discussing congenital and humoral causes, prognosis by age and sex, and preventive/curative powders and distilled waters.

## Page 644 — IIIF Canvas #589

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0589.JP2` (1800 × 2219 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0589.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0589.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Recto page (orig. p. 644, fol. 292r) featuring a watercolor illustration of a young woman spinning at a distaff in the upper left column, followed by a rubricated chapter heading on 'white fever' or chlorosis (Blaichsucht) in young women, detailing its causes in bodily obstructions and its symptoms including pallor, shortness of breath, fatigue, and bizarre pica cravings.

### Illustrations and Anatomical Diagrams

**Young woman spinning with a distaff** (`#xywh=400,400,200,1000`): Watercolor illustration occupying the upper half of the left column, framed at the base by a red and olive-green ruled pedestal border. Depicts a young woman seated in profile facing right on a grey bench, wearing a white cap with red ribbon/feather trim, a white ruffled collar, a green bodice over a white long-sleeved blouse with red cuffs, and a coral-red skirt. She draws a fine thread with her left hand from a golden-yellow distaff mounted on a vertical wooden pole.

### Left Column

| Line | German Diplomatic (Original) | English Translation & Editorial Notes |
| :---: | :--- | :--- |
| 1 | Von Weisen fieber, der Jungk- | Of white fever, of young *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink below the watercolor illustration* |
| 2 | frauen, Blaich Sucht genandt | women, called chlorosis (pale sickness) *— Note: Rubricated heading in red ink* |
| 3 | Das Weibliche geschlecht, ist Neben andern | The female sex is, besides other |
| 4 | gemeinen Kranckheiten villen andern | common illnesses, subjected to many |
| 5 | vnder worffen, welche die Mäner befreid | others from which men are free; |
| 6 | seind, dan es werden wenig Kranckheiten | for there are few illnesses |
| 7 | bey den Mannen gefunden, wo ist die | found among men, where the |
| 8 | Mutter nit darmit am maiste, vnd die eine | womb is not most involved therein, and the one |
| 9 | der andern Kranckheit also Verdünckhlet | obscures the other illness so much |
| 10 | es mag oft hier nit können oder | that one often cannot here |
| 11 | glauben oder auß Nemben khen, woher | believe or discern whence |

| 15 | doch nit all außer der Mutter. in | yet not entirely outside the womb, in |
| 16 | 292 | 292 *— Note: Pencil foliation in bottom left margin* |

## Page 645 — IIIF Canvas #590

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0590.JP2` (1801 × 2017 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0590.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0590.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Verso page (orig. p. 645) continuing the treatise on chlorosis (Blaichsucht), explaining the etiology of pica cravings from rising vapors darkening the brain and imagination, describing harmful dietary habits (raw fruit, cold water, sour milk), prescribing a strict diet, and introducing the section 'Chur und hilff' (Cure and help) with emetics such as Tartarus emeticus, Crocus metallorum, and aqua benedicta.

## Page 661 — IIIF Canvas #598

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0598.JP2` (1801 × 2017 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0598.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0598.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Obstetric and gynecological remedies for pregnant women: guidelines on bloodletting during pregnancy (months 1–3 vs. later months), gentle purging and clysters (cassia, chicken broth, linseed/chamomile oil, manna), survival of infants born in the 7th, 9th, or 10th month vs. 8th month, cooling drinks and purgatives (rhubarb, diacatholicon, tamarind) for fever in pregnancy, and astringent remedies/amulets (dragon's blood, Armenian bole, red coral, sealed earth, bloodstone, amethyst, carnelian) to prevent miscarriage.

## Page 662 — IIIF Canvas #599

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0599.JP2` (1800 × 2223 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0599.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0599.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Continuation of remedies to prevent miscarriage (vervain steam, roasted nutmeg, Vinca pervinca), followed by a rubricated chapter 'Von dem ♀ Emaj' (on excessive menstruation / uterine bleeding): etiology (fermentation and ebullition of blood), normal vs. excessive duration and age of onset (14–16), and a rubricated section 'Chur und Hilff' detailing bloodletting, cupping on the breasts, astringent pills/powders (mastic, Armenian bole, steel, sugar of lead, lapis prunellae, eggshells, Philonium Romanum, Crocus Martis), and a rubricated section 'Volgentes Ist auch Von Großen Krafft' with burnt eggshell lozenges and a black hellebore girdle.

## Page 663 — IIIF Canvas #600

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0600.JP2` (1801 × 2017 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0600.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0600.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Further remedies for excessive menstruation / uterine bleeding: fumigations, foot poultices, sheep's dung powder, a rubricated section 'Volgen noch mehr andere mittel' (belly plaster of ruddle, bloodstone, stag's heart-bone, bole; almond milk, tormentil water, unicorn horn, hartshorn, Diascordium martiale, laudanum opiatum, fresh horse dung juice), a rubricated section 'Volgente Lattwerung Ist ach gar gutt' (conserve of roses, plantain seed, red coral, dragon's blood, Armenian bole, oak bark, burnt blood powder, frankincense steam, coriander seed regimen, yarrow, burnt rhubarb), and a rubricated section 'Köstliche Morsellen in disen Zustand' (morsels of Armenian bole, sealed earth, dragon's blood, cassia lignea, diatubith cum rhabarbaro, and mint water).

## Page 664 — IIIF Canvas #601

**Folio / Side:** recto · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0601.JP2` (2401 × 2994 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0601.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0601.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Continuation of recipes for strengthening the heart and treating fluxes, followed by a major rubricated section on Fluor albus (white flux / leucorrhea) in women, its symptoms, differentiation from gonorrhea, cleansing and purgation treatments, pills, baths, and herbal potions.

## Page 667 — IIIF Canvas #604

**Folio / Side:** verso · **Image:** `b18775275_MS_990_0604.JP2` (2401 × 2689 px) · [IIIF Canvas](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/presentation/b18775275/canvases/b18775275_MS_990_0604.JP2) · [Full-Resolution Image](https://iiif.wellcomecollection.org/image/b18775275_MS_990_0604.JP2/full/full/0/default.jpg) · **Transcription Model:** Gemini 4 Hydrogen · **Tokens (In / Out):** 1,850 / 2,200

Medical recipes and instructions for uterine disorders (Mutterbeschwerden / rising of the womb), including fumigations, remedies using oil and salt of Venus, a precious navel salve, clysters, pessaries, internal potions, remedies when the fetus suffers, and an electuary for rising of the womb.
